# SuperSub Bundles in Mudae

## List of All Known Superbundle-Subbundle Pairs

General ($disableblock-ed) bundles (eg: Anime, Manga...) are excluded from calculations in this file, use the SuperSubGeneral file to see them.

Number of unique super-bundles: 2123 => **2185** (27.1% of all bundles)

Number of unique sub-bundles: 5354 => **5592** (69.2%)
